## `ATG-0321-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ATG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Pathogen Specialist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Seawater Salinity/DO & Fish Viral Pathogen PCR
- **Intro:** Pathogen Specialist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Seawater Salinity/DO & Fish Viral Pathogen PCR — Verify Steady-State Conditions: Salinity: 35.0 ± 0.5 ppt; DO: 6.5 ± 0.2 mg/L; Temperature: 15.0 ± 0.5°C; pH: 8.1 ± 0.1 (if applicable)

- **Country:** `AUS` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Cage Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Deep-Sea Submersible Cage & Feeding
